# Overview — state of the wiki

A short, plain-language snapshot for the owner. Refreshed at each lint pass.

## What's in here now

The wiki holds **1,590 content pages** built from **1,582 raw source files** — the complete backlog (2025-01-01 through mid-2026) plus weekly steady-state intake, now current **through Wednesday October 14 2026** (the 4:47 AM Minsk stand-up of that morning). Everything traces to a source with an inline citation. The 2026-10-14 run (batches 258–262) absorbed **30 recordings = 30 meeting events** from Oct 2 to Oct 14, plus the first monthly **South Dakota VLT performance story** (September 2026) under the Oct-7 scope exception: the daily Minsk stand-ups and Transactional MVP 1 calls, the RIS MVP 1 / MVP 2 planning, the Rage-to-NOVA game conversions (Dragon Pays accepted, Dutch Mill next), the AI project manager going live in Jira on Oct 12, the GitHub↔MSBC bill-of-materials thread with Genadi and Nathan, Larry Sharon's rental pricing, and a busy Oct 13 of outside meetings — Intralot, InComm, ARC Gaming and Bank of America.

By kind (lint pass, 2026-10-14):
- **People — 146** (new this run: Bill Avgeris and Christos Tzoumaras of Intralot — surnames from the notes only; Chris Boncek of the Georgia Lottery — provisional; Byron Booth of ARC). Two new provisional same-name clusters ("Bill", "Chris") carry a shared `disambiguation:` block; all clusters **mechanically checked** this pass.
- **Organizations — 61** (new: **Intralot**, at last its own page; Fontana Amusement).
- **Topics & Concepts — 59**, grouped in the index under 6 sub-headings.
- **Projects — 12.** South Dakota VLT now carries a monthly performance section and timeline.
- **Decisions — 354**, grouped in the index under 5 sub-headings (58 new this run).
- **Meetings — 958.** Thin stubs, grouped by month, complete through **Oct 14 2026**.
- **Open items (frontmatter totals):** 2,223 open commitments and 1,054 open questions across all pages (7 lines quarantined as `blocked-on-*`). The rollup lists below this block are the historical early-batch rollup; the live per-page lists are authoritative.

## Health

- **Run of 2026-10-14 (batches 258–262):** every batch passed the ≥9/10 gate. **138 facts checked across 5 batches — 138/138 held** (26 + 24 + 29 + 23 + 36), 73 of them against Russian source strings. Regression drift-guard: 10 numeric facts re-checked from 2025 pages — **10/10 hold**.
- **Structure (this pass):** index=disk bijection **exact (1,590 = 1,590 = 1,590)**, zero duplicate catalog lines, index "(N sources)" tails match every page, zero dangling links in content pages, artifact-tag scan clean, every raw cited by a page is in that page's Sources list, **0 orphans** (the new Oct-2 ball-recycling decision is reached only from its own meeting stub — no topic page fits it yet). Every raw file is either cited by a page or ledgered in `skipped.md` — nothing staged and forgotten.
- **Same-name clusters:** all owner-ruled clusters and all provisional ones (now incl. "Bill" and "Chris") **pass the mechanical shared-block diff — 1 distinct shared block each** (Kyle and Mauricio pages add page-specific text after an identical block).
- **Privacy:** at staging the run cut family, travel and health small talk from 6 new raws (outside guests at the ARC and Intralot lunches, a banker's illness, a candidate's family); a writer then flagged one more (Val's own age/health remark at Intralot), cut the same way. §15 #9 identifier scan clean on all 61 new files. *Lint finding (owner):* an older page (sean-fellows) quotes a speaker's age ("I'm 63") inside a years-in-business note — under the Sept-28 ruling ages are personal; suggest trimming.
- **Open contradictions are held open, not resolved** (34 pages carry an `## Open contradictions` section). New this run: SAS logs to Grafana by default or not (Oct 2 vs Oct 5); a printer and acceptor in the low-cost street machine (Sept 25 vs Val to Intralot Oct 13); Primero's up-front Class A fee ($1,000 / $1,150–1,250 / $1,500); Kyle Shepherd's yes-then-no.
- **The standing data-quality finding** stands: the derived notes keep altering their source (this run: "RAID" for Rage, "Peach 1+2" where Felipe corrected it, VP8 vs VP9). The transcript remains the fact.

- **Scope.** Three separate May-25 meetings. **E2E Testing Framework Evolution** — notes-only, header 10:02 AM ET, ~1h32m, Russian; the ten-person working group (Val/Pavel Frolov/Alexander Biratko/Irina Shishko/Evgeny Kharetski sick-audio-only/Dzmitry Chudzinau/Alexey Khamin/Ivan Shpak/Sergey Tsimanov/Vladimir Samusenko). **Transactional Products Review — Flex-IQ, services** — transcript-only, header 12:05 PM ET, ~1h40m, RU/EN garbled; Pavel Frolov/Andrei Klimovich/Irina Shishko/Val. **GSM/GPS Modem and Event Recorder** — transcript-only, header 3:30 PM ET (filename `_113031` UTC ~4h behind ignored), ~43m, RU/EN garbled; Val/Pavel Frolov/Genadi Peari.
- **Content — E2E.** Meeting #1 of the 3-meeting "choking meeting" cycle set on May-22; the working group evolving the transactional E2E approach. Pavel's four status-quo problems + his AI-agent-that-performs-QA direction; Alexander's no-universal-framework position (Playwright is the universal protocol; common pieces as imported libraries); Val's reframe (it's an *approach* not a framework; feed the AI the v5.1 data contracts and let it compose tests; QA is historical inertia); Dzmitry's platform-scope E2E view (terminal + ~10 servers + Cerberus/Kulak + Business Central + Twilio + hardware; check logs reach Grafana); the cross-repo CI-trigger problem (Andrei Bosik's origin question, no conclusion); observability + "who tests the tests?" (screenshots + logs + critic agent); hallucination = LLM probability; agent personas + memory for negative testing. Decisions: no fixed framework, transactional product as testbed, independent research → follow-up Wed May 27 6 PM ET.
- **Content — Flex-IQ.** Pavel walks Andrei through IQ at engineering depth (the mechanics behind the May-22 MVP strategy). POS→IQ rename; device-registration/encrypted-channel security (Scanner app double-scan; per-device secret keys); Moo the Goose printer-intercept + weak "confidence number" validation (Moo is the point of truth); redemption two ways — gift card (Incom, ~$2 fee, ~$7 min) or lottery tickets (Georgia $5 cash-outs → void + $5 lottery); split not built for July 5; the copy-ticket print question (Val wants NO auto-print, block by July 5); protective-glass player-screen problem parked (pick test locations without glass; move screen to dispenser); PIN model + two roles; no emulator (devs mock); Val's "zero new features before July 5" ruling (collect big list after ~10–20 beta sites → file ~September).
- **Content — Modem/Event-Recorder.** Exploratory, no decisions. LTE modem ~$10, plugs into board (FP7/PS7 full slot, FP1000 smaller, maybe USB on Flex), + SIM = wireless over GSM (Pavel tested on FlexPOS); cell-tower positioning (~half km) vs a separate GPS antenna; standalone GPS trackers are closed Chinese cloud (can't tap); ~200 MB/mo data plan, cloned SIMs / one-account-per-bank to cut cost (each machine autonomous; data-collection only, not progressive comms); always-on-when-off deferred (needs separate processor+battery). Event recorder ("black box"): external box duplicates the machine (lab-only) → use own SSD/RAM/modem; storage via SATA/PCIe/USB, liked FRAM/battery-RAM with power-warning (~150 ms); GSM largely solves the log-delivery gap; hard part = *what* to record. Actions: Genadi searches ready modules + fast storage; Val formulates carrier-quote request.
- **Routing.** E2E → [[wiki/topics/github-release-management-pipeline.md]] (new "May 25 2026" section, continuing the transactional-E2E thread — reused, links back to May-22) + [[wiki/topics/ai-assisted-development.md]] (new "May 25 2026" node — agent-performs-QA / personas / critic agent). Flex-IQ → [[wiki/topics/flexpay-business-architecture-payout-devices.md]] (new "May 25 2026" section — the FlexIQ/transactional home; reused, links back to May-22 MVP). Modem → [[wiki/topics/flexpay-business-architecture-payout-devices.md]] (new "May 25 2026" section). **No new topic invented.** Modem decision (reported): the GSM/GPS-modem + event-recorder subject is a real durable hardware subsystem, but this is the FIRST substantive meeting on it, it reached NO decisions (all exploratory research assignments), only ONE inbound link is available, and it directly extends an existing open commitment (the Flex-Recycler cellular-modem-with-GPS question) plus the ARM/Linux board decision's interface set — so the ≥3-inbound durable-subject bar is not met; routed to the flexpay device home. ### Batch 165 journal (2026-07-30) — May-26/27 2026: THREE distinct notes-only meetings → THREE stubs (all sc-1)
- **Scope.** Three loose single files, all NOTES-ONLY. **Flex-IQ MVP Cashier & Player Flow Demo** — header 3:02 PM rec-start, ~1h9m, Russian (Andrei Klimovich/Pavel Frolov/Irina Shishko/Val; Lesha Khamin + Vanya Shpak on Minsk hardware). **Cloud Back-end Platform Architecture** — header 27 May 12:35 PM ET, ~1h44m (Val/Dzmitry Chudzinau/Andrei Bosik/Pavel Frolov/Sergey Tsimanov/Irina Shishko/Igor Metelski/Alexander Biratko/Anton Leshkevich/Evgeny Kharetski). **Testing Framework E2E** — header 27 May 10:05 AM ET, ~1h43m (the full E2E working group: Val/Pavel Frolov/Andrei Bosik/Alexander Biratko/Irina Shishko/Dzmitry Chudzinau/Vladimir Samusenko/Sergey Tsimanov/Ivan Shpak/Alexey Khamin/Evgeny Kharetski).
- **Content — Flex-IQ demo (May-26).** Live end-to-end MVP demo (install→customer/location setup→InComm approval→Payment-Hub funding→cashier payout) + assembled hardware on camera. Roles not built yet (all-JVL full access); InComm approval is manual email + hand copy-paste of sandbox test cards; shuffling-PIN keypad; **card did NOT issue** (InComm side failed + wrong machine/voucher-number field) = main blocker; both Andrei+Pavel locked out (Keycloak has no password recovery); FLEX-POS in-line splice (two DB9) between Mutha Goose and printer + green RS-485 daisy-chain; Andrei's counter-vs-behind-counter cable-walkway constraint (~1.6m; RS-232 extender vs wireless); T&C PDF is a placeholder (Juan drafting). Decisions: proceed-to-MVP (subject to fix list), no-abbreviations-ever (house style), defer-UX-nice-to-haves; fix list into Jira (clearer cashout display + QWERTY keypad by July 5, Keycloak recovery, ticket voucher-number, Help-screen contacts).
- **Content — Cloud Back-end (May-27).** Dzmitry's ~15-service cloud catalog for the new land-based machine platform (gateway, Keycloak login, user/operator mgmt, machine registration, a new **manifest registry** each software piece publishes to, RS-config, QR service, per-spin event collector, progressive, financial reporting replacing "Palapku Bam"[garbled]+Tableau, license/jurisdiction, LGS client). Deferred: legacy migration, server-side barcode (metal-plate lead time), data retention. Gaming-web-services product-owner open (Klimovich busy, Metelski ill, Lesha Khamin proposed); transactional ships next week, ~90% patterns shared.
- **Content — E2E meeting #2 (May-27).** The full working group's "choking-cycle" meeting #2 (distinct from the May-22 origin + May-25 meeting #1). When is a CICD-built product "done"? — validating the transaction system E2E across many self-contained repos (closest to Standard 5.1). Three positions (Pavel's over-repo dependency-graph control layer vs "build a test platform first" vs Andrei Bosik's already-existing integration repo + auto-generated `docs-contract/`); Val's don't-scaffold-the-AI push (Karpathy propose-correct-accumulate; stop theorizing, try a real release); **contracts are one-sided/zero-trust**; **Standard 5.1 officially released** company-wide (Biratko distributes, Bosik attaches compliance-report link); pivot = attempt a real production release next week (excl. Nathan's MSBC/billing/location) to surface real problems (secrets mgmt, domain names, DB strategy), Friday May-29 repurposed; CLAUDE.md-scale-limit → per-repo wiki/Obsidian (the second-brain-adjacent note).

### Batch 166 journal (2026-07-30) — May-28/29 2026: TWO distinct notes-only meetings → TWO stubs (both sc-1)
- **Scope.** Two loose single files, both NOTES-ONLY, no README/media. **QA Strategy / AI Development Cycle** — header 28 May 2:31 PM ET, ~1h7m, Russian (Val/Pavel Frolov/Irina Shishko). **AI Testing And Build Architecture** — header 29 May 10:03 AM ET, ~1h, Russian (Val/Andrei Bosik/Pavel Frolov/Irina Shishko/Evgeny Kharetski). Both continue the E2E/testing-framework "choking-cycle" thread (May-22 origin → May-25 #1 → May-27 E2E #2 → these).
- **Content — QA Strategy (May-28).** Open discussion, **no firm process settled**, on where QA/testing fits the AI-driven dev cycle. Irina = team still on old testing approach, still fitting GitHub repos to the new process. Pavel = the AI (Claude Code) already runs many tests on real machines and logs them in PR/code-review notes but they aren't saved as a repeatable plan → invisible to QA. Val's idea = a Claude Code "skill" to capture the AI's own runs into an agreed, reusable test-case format (no such skill yet). Pavel's "bricks" idea (credited to Andrei) = AI writes small reusable positive/negative regression tests into a dedicated test repo, assembles E2E, checks conflicts. Decisions: tie QA to **data-contract changes + associated actions** (Val; Pavel agrees at single-step scale, warns timing/randomness/human-input escape); **run tests on the PR in sequence** (integration → E2E → merge); **don't build a large human-managed test DB** (let ~500 tests run day/night; Elon Musk "too capable to supervise" line). Contract-check timing unresolved (Irina early vs Pavel at integration).
- **Content — AI Testing & Build Architecture (May-29).** "Think it through" session (team paused forward work until direction settled) on how products are tested AND built. Decisions: **drop deterministic frameworks** (wrong direction; constant integration testing shrinks E2E); **E2E needn't boot the whole machine**; **AI testing bounded & operator-controlled** (AI proposes negative scenarios → team curates); **forward-only versioning** as discipline (mainly gaming; LeeX/IQ fine; old SVN excluded); **rename "components"→"artifacts"**; **v5.1 repo standard accepted ~80%**; **Monday Jun 1 session** to reach a written standard. Build-ownership map (no global architecture owner; transactional build actions mostly Sergey Simonov; gaming split — LGS by Kharetski+Biratko, OS+ISO by Pavel, packaging by Biratko; installer = separate CI/CD project → SD-card image). The gap = installer doesn't check at build time whether blocks work together → Andrei's fix = every API repo emits a data contract as build-step output; Evgeny's preferred manifest-in-integration-repo approach. Test taxonomy unresolved (Andrei to share a Habr testing-pyramid article).
- **Routing.** Both → [[wiki/topics/github-release-management-pipeline.md]] (new "May 28 2026" + "May 29 2026" sections, continuing the transactional-E2E/build thread) AND [[wiki/topics/ai-assisted-development.md]] (new "May 28 2026" — capture-into-a-skill + don't-over-manage; new "May 29 2026" — bounded AI testing + testing-pyramid). **No new topic invented** — both fit the two existing topic pages (reused per instruction).
- **§12 (mandatory, corpus-wide).** Both raws are NEW — neither cited in any content page, neither in [[wiki/skipped.md]], no pre-existing 2026-05-28/29 meeting page. Confirmed DISTINCT sessions from the May-27 E2E #2 and each other: different dates, different attendee sets (May-28 = 3 people; May-29 = 5 incl. Bosik + Kharetski), different durations, and non-overlapping decisions (May-28 = QA-to-data-contracts + PR test sequence; May-29 = build-ownership map, installer gap, manifest, forward-only versioning, artifacts rename, v5.1 ~80%). Neither is a re-transcription. **No §12 skips this batch; no README/media** (two loose single files).

Substance = **Unbind ≠ Freeze** (two separate independent functions; the ops dashboard's forced freeze-before-unbind sequence rejected — freeze = reversible operational stop e.g. non-payment, can auto-reactivate; unbind = detach unit from location); **never silently rebind** (a unit reappearing at a different location = alarm/possible fraud; a removed unit can never return to its old location; reuse always needs a fresh connection at a new location → removes manual unbind-then-rebind labour, fixes scaling); **offline units issue no cards, only printed tickets** (card issuable only at the millisecond the button is pressed while online; only the button-press state matters); **invalid pending transactions on a detached/decommissioned unit (could be ~10) are deleted / hard-reset** since they can never finalize (both agreed); the harder question → Val's **escrow-style hold** idea (MuthaGoose holds until IQ validates) pending MuthaGoose's command protocol he doesn't have (a question for Pavel), then he left for an America meeting and resumed in the 7:05pm call. Routed → flexpay-business-architecture-payout-devices (new "Jul 21 2026 — unbind vs freeze / offline transactions" section placed chronologically BEFORE the escrow section; sc 145→147). Also updated the escrow section's forward reference from a vague "tracked elsewhere" to a direct link to this new stub. Reused existing homes; no new pages. §15.8 clear — unbind/freeze/offline-transaction/device life-cycle economics only; NO named-person compensation. §4: Val→val-levitan.md, Andrei Klimovich→andrei-klimovich.md both on disk; "Dmitry" (the case originator) handled on the sibling escrow page as [[wiki/people/dmitri-chudinov.md|Dmitri Chudinov]] and not re-linked here (this stub cites the protocol question to Pavel per the source); all link targets verified, zero dangling. ### §17 STEADY-STATE — ACTIVATED 2026-08-03 (routine steady-state-jvl-brain created; Fri 4PM)
Owner rulings 2026-08-03: (1) Teams chats OUT of scope — codified in CLAUDE.md §1; (2) propose the auto-trigger mechanism before activating; (3) weekly report auto-send to val@jvl.ca AUTHORIZED — codified in CLAUDE.md §17.1.
PROPOSED MECHANISM (not yet created — awaiting owner OK):
- Scheduler: one entry in the Claude Code scheduled-tasks scheduler (~/.claude/scheduled-tasks/<id>/SKILL.md — the canonical, only scheduler per user global CLAUDE.md). Survives reboots (daemon-level).
- Schedule: Friday 4:00 PM machine-local time.
- Boot header: SKILL.md opens by reading the second-brain engine (this repo's CLAUDE.md) since the scheduler launches outside the project folder and won't auto-discover it.
- Single-writer: repo lockfile .steady-state.lock (pid+ISO-timestamp). On wake: if a fresh lock exists → exit (a run is still going); else write lock at start, remove at end (+ stale-lock override if >6h old).
- §9 FIRST: before staging anything, run the interrupted-batch recovery (staged-but-uncommitted raws / bijection check → Path A finish-close-out or Path B reset).
- The batch: normal weekly pending-sweep ingest — staging+md5, corpus-wide §12, writer/verifier split, ≥10-fact verify, §15.8 guard, Teams-chat/email/PII §1 exclusions, month-close manifest at month boundaries, monthly §13 lint, qmd update+embed, commit+push as ValLevitan.
- Weekly report: plain prose to val@jvl.ca, auto-sent (now authorized). Environment caveat: headless/cron runs may lack the interactively-authed ms365 MCP — if send is unavailable that run, write the report to a fixed path (wiki/weekly-report-latest.md) and flag it; never skip. Failure leads with "This week's update did NOT complete".
- Review clause: after 4 runs, report runs-needing-help / avg batch size / cadence misses.

### Batch 237 (June 2026 back-fill — 6 events, 11 files) — appended 2026-09-19
Weekly steady-state run. SIX June-2026 meeting events that had never been staged: Val's **Nebraska/South Dakota field trip** (J&J Gaming Omaha site visit + its two continuation sessions, Funambulist Gaming Omaha, MG Oil Rapid City) plus the **Stephen Fenoglio** Texas-counsel introductory call. Their files were back-filed into the vault month folder on 2026-09-07 and 2026-09-11 — after June closed and after the batches that processed June ran.
**Process finding (the important one):** the previous weekly run swept only the CURRENT month folder, so it could not see these. The meeting pipeline demonstrably back-files into closed months, therefore **the weekly pending sweep must diff the whole in-scope tree**. Done that way this run; the fix is in the routine's practice, and the §9.127 month-close manifest cannot be relied on alone because files arrive after the manifest is written.
**Corpus corrections this batch earned its keep on.** The J&J transcript (new) was checked against the J&J notes (ingested in July) and falsified several things the wiki had been asserting: two machine prices recorded as J&J's numbers were actually **Val's guesses that Rick Rees did not confirm**; the **$77 net-revenue-per-unit-per-day figure is a 13-day window**, not a standing average; and the "SMIB = Slot Machine Interface Board" expansion appears in **neither** source. Separately the June-18 MG Oil source **predates and falsifies two "first/only in the corpus" superlatives** the wiki had placed on September-2026 sources — the first naming of "Passport" and the only offer to remove the reset control. All corrected in place.
**Tier A — queued for the owner:** (1) `wiki/people/troy-erikson.md` is spelled **Erikson** but the June-18 notes and the state operator list both say **Erickson** — rename candidate, not done mid-run. (2) **Axcess vs Access Entertainment** (South Dakota route operator) needs a spelling ruling, plus confirmation it is a different entity from the Georgia card vendor the corpus carries as "Access".
**Tier B:** the Funambulist and steakhouse transcripts both carry a recurring Plaud diarization artifact ("Mark Garland" as a speaker label for a person the notes do not list) — recorded as an artifact, no person page created. Val's own figures in the steakhouse conversation are internally inconsistent (67% vs 65% combined tax; 15–20% vs 25%) and are recorded as spoken, unreconciled.
**§15.8 holds (steakhouse lunch, a personal conversation):** personal matters and a pay characterisation were deliberately not recorded; exclusion notes sit on the pages.
**Source-defect handling:** the steakhouse transcript's speaker labels are **inverted** (Plaud attributed Val's lines to Rick Rees and Rick's to "Speaker 1"); the swap was applied to every attributed fact and the defect is declared on the stub.
**Not staged (out of scope):** the derived notes reference Obsidian visit notes (`2026-06-16 Funambulist Gaming`, `2026-06-18 MG Oil`, `2026-06-18 TNT Wagner`) that are vault notes rather than meeting transcripts — §1 keeps them out of scope. The TNT Wagner note is the only evidence the Whitey Tolliver / Mike Kotab introduction actually happened, so that introduction is recorded as the notes' claim, not as verified fact.
